  │ 207 +                                                                  │
  │ 208 + def check_error_path_messages() -> InvariantCheck:               │
  │ 209 +     """                                                          │
  │ 210 +     Check that error paths have user-facing messages.            │
  │ 211 +                                                                  │
  │ 212 +     Returns:                                                     │
  │ 213 +         InvariantCheck with pass/fail status                     │
  │ 214 +     """                                                          │
  │ 215 +     # Many error paths just log or silently fail                 │
  │ 216 +     all_paths_have_messages = False  # From source analysis      │
  │ 217 +                                                                  │
  │ 218 +     return InvariantCheck(                                       │
  │ 219 +         invariant_id="IV-DH-006",                                │
  │ 220 +         description="Every error path must have a user-facing m… │
  │ 221 +         passed=all_paths_have_messages,                          │
  │ 222 +         evidence="Error paths exist without corresponding user … │
  │ 223 +         recommended_fix="Add user-facing error messages to all … │
  │ 224 +     )                                                            │
  │ 225 +                                                                  │
  │ 226 +                                                                  │
  │ 227 + # -------------------------------------------------------------… │
  │ 228 + # Composite check runner                                         │
  │ 229 + # -------------------------------------------------------------… │
  │ 230 +                                                                  │
  │ 231 +                                                                  │
  │ 232 + def run_all_invariant_checks() -> Tuple[InvariantCheck, ...]:    │
  │ 233 +     """Run all 6 invariant checks and return results."""         │
  │ 234 +     return (                                                     │
  │ 235 +         check_tick_budget_compliance(),                          │
  │ 236 +         check_queue_boundedness(),                               │
  │ 237 +         check_gl_context_guard(),                                │
  │ 238 +         check_thread_context_before_gl(),                        │
  │ 239 +         check_config_validation_warning(),                       │
  │ 240 +         check_error_path_messages(),                             │
  │ 241 +     )                                                            │
  │ 242 +                                                                  │
  │ 243 +                                                                  │
  │ 244 + def get_invariant_summary() -> Dict[str, Any]:                   │
  │ 245 +     """Get a summary of all invariant checks."""                 │
  │ 246 +     checks = run_all_invariant_checks()                          │
  │ 247 +     passed = sum(1 for c in checks if c.passed)                  │
  │ 248 +     failed = len(checks) - passed                                │
  │ 249 +                                                                  │
  │ 250 +     return {                                                     │
  │ 251 +         "domain": "D_DH_STANDALONE",                             │
  │ 252 +         "total_checks": len(checks),                             │
  │ 253 +         "passed": passed,                                        │
  │ 254 +         "failed": failed,                                        │
  │ 255 +         "all_passed": failed == 0,                               │
  │ 256 +         "violations": [                                          │
  │ 257 +             {                                                    │
  │ 258 +                 "id": c.invariant_id,                            │
  │ 259 +                 "description": c.description,                    │
  │ 260 +                 "location": c.violation_location,                │
  │ 261 +                 "fix": c.recommended_fix,                        │
  │ 262 +             }                                                    │
  │ 263 +             for c in checks if not c.passed                      │
  │ 264 +         ],                                                       │
  │ 265 +     }                                                            │
  ╰────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
